With FEDOS_BETA_DIRECT_RATCHET_MESSAGE_DELIVERY_REMOTE_DEVICE_REF deliberately left UNSET (the real, ordinary default every actual user launch uses), acceptance-send-message still returned outcome="ok" (messageId=d79772585b084595b28f0951ddad679f) via the legacy route -- proving sendMessage() routes independently of live Ratchet session state entirely, never consulting it. The real "downgrade-detected" no-downgrade gate lives inside resolveFedosDirectRatchetRoute() (fedos_direct_ratchet_route_resolution.ts:108-119) but is only reachable FROM the ratchetDelivery thunk itself, so it can never fire on the code path an ordinary send actually takes. This is a pre-existing, already-disclosed architectural gap (see this file's own header) -- NOT something the identity-space unification arc fixes; fixing it would mean redesigning the send-time route decision, out of that arc's own mandate.

The real "downgrade-detected" no-downgrade gate lives inside resolveFedosDirectRatchetRoute() (fedos_direct_ratchet_route_resolution.ts:108-119) but is only reachable FROM the ratchetDelivery thunk itself, so it can never fire on the code path an ordinary send actually takes.
